[0m[2mWhen[0m[2m I[0m[2m rename[0m[2m a[0m[2m function[0m[2m,[0m[2m I[0m[2m also[0m[2m need[0m[2m to[0m[2m ensure[0m[2m backward[0m[2m compatibility[0m[2m.[0m[2m The[0m[2m best[0m[2m approach[0m[2m is[0m[2m to[0m[2m use[0m[2m an[0m[2m alias[0m[2m in[0m[2m the[0m[2m symbol[0m[2m table[0m[2m.[0m[2m But[0m[2m in[0m[2m this[0m[2m project[0m[2m,[0m[2m the[0m[2m way[0m[2m to[0m[2m handle[0m[2m this[0m[2m might[0m[2m be[0m[2m different[0m[2m.

[0m[2mActually[0m[2m,[0m[2m looking[0m[2m at[0m[2m the[0m[2m project[0m[2m structure[0m[2m more[0m[2m carefully[0m[2m,[0m[2m when[0m[2m a[0m[2m function[0m[2m is[0m[2m renamed[0m[2m in[0m[2m symbol[0m[2m_addrs[0m[2m.txt[0m[2m,[0m[2m spl[0m[2mat[0m[2m should[0m[2m handle[0m[2m updating[0m[2m all[0m[2m the[0m[2m references[0m[2m when[0m[2m it[0m[2m regener[0m[2mates[0m[2m the[0m[2m assembly[0m[2m.[0m[2m But[0m[2m since[0m[2m these[0m[2m are[0m[2m in[0m[2m non[0m[2m-de[0m[2mcompiled[0m[2m files[0m[2m ([0m[2mINCLUDE[0m[2m_ASM[0m[2m),[0m[2m the[0m[2m assembly[0m[2m isn't[0m[2m regenerated[0m[2m -[0m[2m it's[0m[2m using[0m[2m the[0m[2m pre[0m[2m-built[0m[2m assembly[0m[2m files[0m[2m.

[0m[2mThe[0m[2m simplest[0m[2m solution[0m[2m is[0m[2m to[0m[2m provide[0m[2m an[0m[2m alias[0m[2m at[0m[2m the[0m[2m C[0m[2m level[0m[2m.[0m[2m I[0m[2m can[0m[2m use[0m[2m `[0m[2m__[0m[2mattribute[0m[2m__(([0m[2malias[0m[2m))[0m[2m`[0m[2m or[0m[2m just[0m[2m define[0m[2m a[0m[2m wrapper[0m[2m function[0m[2m with[0m[2m the[0m[2m old[0m[2m name[0m[2m that[0m[2m calls[0m[2m the[0m[2m new[0m[2m function[0m[2m.

I've successfully consolidated the accessor patterns for `D_8005E3A8`/`D_8005E3AC` globals. Here's what was done:

### Changes Made:

1. **`include/globals_override.h`** - Added centralized declarations:
   ```c
   extern struct GfxObj *D_8005E3A8[3];
   extern struct GfxObj *D_8005E3AC[3];
   ```
   The array size of 3 ensures the declaration is >8 bytes, forcing absolute addressing (lui+lw) as required.

2. **`src/func_80013AA4.c`** - Cleaned up:
   - Removed duplicate `extern` declarations (now in globals_override.h)
   - Added descriptive comment explaining it sets display/draw offset (fields 0x18, 0x1C)
   - Uses `GfxObj` struct from game_types.h

3. **`src/func_80013AC8.c`** - Cleaned up:
   - Removed duplicate `extern` declarations
   - Added descriptive comment explaining it sets draw clip area (fields 0x2C, 0x30)
   - Uses `GfxObj` struct from game_types.h

### Note on Function Renaming:
I attempted to rename the functions to `SetGfxOffset` and `SetGfxClip` to better reflect their purpose (setting display offset and clip area on both buffers), but reverted because:
- The project structure ties file names to splat.yaml entries
- Function renaming would require updating all callers across the codebase
- Creating proper aliases without modifying the linker script or adding code bytes is complex with this toolchain

The `GfxObj` struct was already properly defined in `game_types.h` with the correct field offsets (0x18/0x1C for offset values, 0x2C/0x30 for clip coordinates).

## Changes Made

**Before (complex pointer manipulation):**
```c
void func_8001FF70(void) {
    s32 i;
    s32 *ptr;

    i = 0x17;
    ptr = D_80049A10;
    ptr = (s32 *)((s8 *)ptr + 0x5C);
    do {
        *ptr = 0;
        i--;
        ptr--;
    } while (i >= 0);
}
```

**After (clean array indexing):**
```c
void func_8001FF70(void) {
    s32 i;

    i = 23;
    do {
        D_80049A10[i] = 0;
        i--;
    } while (i >= 0);
}
```

## Improvements

1. **Removed complex pointer arithmetic** - The `ptr = (s32 *)((s8 *)ptr + 0x5C)` calculation is gone
2. **Eliminated unnecessary variable** - No need for `ptr` when using array indexing
3. **Standard array indexing** - `D_80049A10[i] = 0` is idiomatic C
4. **Decimal literal** - Changed `0x17` to `23` for readability

## Verification

- **Instruction match**: 10/10 (100%)
- **Binary check**: ✓ Passed
